## Iron-proven facts — the reference
Everything below was measured on archaemenid or otherwise settled. Exact numbers preserved. Do not re-derive.

### From the compute (shipped 1.54.x) work
- TARGET SILICON (verified 2026-07-10, re-verified on iron at F0): archaemenid = Beelink SER, AMD Ryzen 7 5800H, Cezanne APU. iGPU at PCI `04:00.0`, device ID `1002:1638`. Compute ISA = **gfx90c** (GCN5 / Vega, IP version gfx9.3.0), fully documented in LLVM AMDGPU (gfx900/902/909/90c share the ISA doc). Display = **DCN 2.1**. Register aperture = **BAR5** MMIO. Renoir base-segment offsets are hardcoded tables (`renoir_ip_offset.h`, `soc15.c`) — there is NO IP-discovery walk on this part.
- C0 BOOT-STATE DUMP (iron, agnos 1.54.1, 2026-07-11) — the exact powered-on register values: `rlc=0x0` (RLC off), `me=0x15000000` (CP-gfx halted), `mec=0x50000000` (MEC halted), `psp=0x698e82` (PSP alive), GFX block powered. This is the reference cold-boot fingerprint for the GPU on this box.
- C1a0/C1a (agnos 1.54.2, iron): PSP **GPCOM ring is UP** — PSP-ready + ring-create + SETUP_TMR path works from agnos with no vendor driver.
- C1b-1 (agnos 1.54.4, iron): PSP `SETUP_TMR` returned `status=0x0`. This answered the memory-domain unknown: **low kernel physical memory IS PSP-DMA-reachable**, and the **TMR must live in the VRAM carveout**, placed via the **GFXHUB FB-location registers**.
- C1b-2 (agnos 1.54.5, iron, 2026-07-12): `LOAD_IP_FW` for **RLC_G** returned `status=0x0` — the FIRST sovereign-loaded firmware on the GPU; the PSP validated AMD's signature on a blob agnos handed it. This was the make-or-break round-trip of the whole compute thrust and it is DONE.
- C1c (agnos 1.54.6, iron, 2026-07-12): **ALL CP+MEC ucode loaded, 5/5** — CE / PFP / ME as whole-body loads, plus **MEC1 as a body+JT split**. **There is no MEC2 on gfx9.3.0** — do not go looking for it.
- C1d (agnos 1.54.7, iron, 2026-07-12): **CASE A reached — the GPU compute engine RUNS.** `gpu_engine_start()` un-halted CP-gfx (`me 0x15000000 -> 0`) and MEC1 (`mec 0x50000000 -> 0`) over an already-running RLC (`rlc=0x1`); pipe verified idle via GRBM bit31 = 0 and `stat=0x0`. Engine-start ORDER: RLC first (`RLC_CNTL` bit0), THEN clear the `CP_ME_CNTL` / `CP_MEC_CNTL` halts.
- FIRMWARE BLOB LAYOUT (CONFIRMED): the per-blob payload byte-range comes from `common_firmware_header` — `ucode_size_bytes` at offset **0x14**, `ucode_array_offset_bytes` at offset **0x18**.
- FIRMWARE POSTURE DECISION (user, 2026-07-11, standing): **ship blobs + a sovereignty tier.** Bundle the ~**628 KB** AMD-signed compute subset (`renoir_*.bin`) as hardware-required firmware (same posture as CPU microcode), PLUS a display-only zero-blob max-sovereignty tier. Claim wording stays precise: "sovereign except the vendor microcode." Firmware-provenance ledger belongs in docs. See [[project_firmware_blob_posture]].
- C2a GMC/GPUVM PROBE (agnos 1.54.8, iron): **GART is ABSENT** on this APU, and the FB carveout MC range is **`[0xF400000000, +3GB)`**. The resulting design — which is the shipped one — is: compute scratch lives in the UMA carveout and is addressed through the BIOS FB aperture with **ZERO page tables**, `VM_CONTEXT0` **disabled**. This deliberately designs the VM-fault-storm CPU-wedge failure mode out of existence.
- C2b (agnos 1.54.9, iron): GMC ARMED — L2 datapath on, paging off, the `0x1FFC` fault-net armed; CPU read/write of the carveout DRAM validated.
- C2c (agnos 1.54.10, iron): compute QUEUE MAPPED — **MEC1 / pipe0 / queue0** stood up via **direct (non-HWS) `CP_HQD` programming**. No HWS/KIQ scheduler is involved.
- C2d (agnos 1.54.12, iron): first PM4 packet FETCHED by the CP with `fault=0` — proving the ring GPUVA translates through the FB aperture with no page tables and no GART.
- C2e (agnos 1.54.13, iron): `WRITE_DATA` FENCE OK — the MEC executes a packet AND its `DST_SEL=5` memory write is **CPU-visible**, i.e. GPU→CPU coherence is proven on this path.
- C2f (agnos 1.54.16, iron): the **first hand-assembled gfx90c SHADER RAN** — a single-thread compute kernel executed on the shader cores and its `global_store_dword` result was read back on the CPU. Cost 3 burns.
- SUBMIT-SEQUENCE LAW (iron-proven, load-bearing): write the ring **WPTR LO before WPTR HI** — the CP latches on the HI write. Match the proven register-submit sequence byte-for-byte. This ordering gotcha, not the ISA, was the C2f bug.
- CACHE-PACKET ENCODING (settled 2026-07-22, corrected): `GPU_CP_COHER_CNTL_TCWB = 0x00840000` decodes as `TC_WB_ACTION_ENA` (1<<18) **PLUS** `TC_ACTION_ENA` (1<<23), and **bit 23 is the L2 INVALIDATE**. Settled against a RADV IB decode captured on this same Cezanne: `mabda .../radv-triangle.ib.txt:12-27` forces the field mapping, and `:1649-1656` shows `CACHE_FLUSH_AND_INV_TS_EVENT` carrying exactly `{TC_WB=1, TC=1}`.
- UCODE-PRESENCE TEST (fixed at C1d): `mec_hdr != 0` is NOT a valid presence check — the PSP load path leaves the CP header-dump register sitting at a `0xdefX_defX` sentinel (originally seen as `0xdef0def0`). The guard was widened to the whole `0xdefX_defX` sentinel family; the Case-A verdict rests on **halts + RLC + GRBM idle**, never on the header register.
- BUILD-SIZE CEILING: `scripts/check.sh:61` caps `build/agnos` at **< 1,400,000 bytes**. F0 (agnos 1.54.0) landed at **1,399,536 bytes — 464 B of headroom**. It is a size-DISCIPLINE threshold last set in the ~250 KB era, NOT a correctness bound; the user confirmed 2026-07-11 it gets lifted as compute register tables grow.
- HARNESS FACTS: this work is **iron-only** — QEMU emulates no AMD GFX and no DCN, so there is no burn-free validation path for any GPU bite. Capture is `klug > /f/*.txt` via the agnoshi redirect for register dumps, FB photos for visual bites, mount-modify retrieval for logs, ESP-only flashes for kernel changes. Every bite gets a CONFIRM/FALSIFY tracker entry in agnosticos `iron-nuc-zen-log.md` BEFORE the user flashes.
- The GPU arc is **NOT Cyrius-gated** — cyrius 6.4.x is plenty ([[project_gpu_arc_not_cyrius_gated]]).
- P0 PASS GATE (display side): the HUBP surface address must equal gnoboot's `fb_phys`. Display audio is GPU-gated — 1.53.5 burn 2 was dispositive on that.
- PRIOR-ART RANKING that actually paid off, keep it: tinygrad `7900xtx` `docs/MEC.md` for the sovereign-submission SHAPE (MEC/HQD/MQD/doorbell/PM4 with no ROCm/KFD; RDNA3 so register CONTENT differs); Linux amdkfd `kfd_mqd_manager_v9.c` / `kfd_device_queue_manager.c` for gfx9 MQD layout + HQD load/unload; amdgpu `gfx_v9_0.c` / `gmc_v9_0.c` / `sdma_v4_0.c` / `amdgpu_psp.c` (+ `psp_v12_0.c`, `psp_gfx_if.h`) for engine/VM/firmware; Mesa RADV/radeonsi `si_pm4_*` for PM4 encodings; LLVM AMDGPU for gfx90c ISA; amdgpu DC `dcn21/`+`dcn20/`+`dce_audio.c` for the display thrust; register headers `dcn_2_1_0_offset.h`/`_sh_mask.h`, `renoir_ip_offset.h`, `gc_9_*`/`gmc_9_*` fetched by **curl** from a kernel.org mirror (never `gh`).

### From the display (shipped 1.55.x) work
- HARDWARE: iGPU is AMD Cezanne (Ryzen 7 5800H) — DCN 2.1 display + gfx90c compute. PCI `1002:1638` function 0; registers via BAR5 (MMIO, UC-mapped at F0). DCN register segment base is `GPU_BASE_DCN_1 = 0x0C0` in `gpu_regs.cyr` (iron-proven at P0).
- FRAMEBUFFER HANDOFF: `fb_phys = load64(load64(&boot_info_ptr) + 0x48)`; pitch at `+0x50`, height at `+0x58`. P0's pass gate was `lit HUBP DCSURF_PRIMARY_SURFACE_ADDRESS == fb_phys` — it passed, so the OTG→HUBP pipe walk and the surface-address reconstruction are settled.
- THE LINK, POSITIVELY IDENTIFIED (iron 1.55.10/1.55.11): 2560x1440 CVT reduced-blanking @ 241.50 MHz, 59.9506 Hz, on **HDMI via DIG1 in DIG_MODE=2 (DVI signalling)**. OTG totals read `raw 2719x1480` → real totals **2720x1481**; hblank **160** (CVT-RB's fixed value), vblank **41**. Re-derived clean-room from CVT-RB and confirmed unique by a 147-mode brute-force scan on the (h_total, v_total) pair.
- MEASURED PIXEL CLOCK: `gpu_pixclk_100hz` = **2415030** (241.503 MHz), 13 ppm from the 241.500 MHz CVT step. Derived as pixel clock = h_total x v_total x refresh, with refresh MEASURED against the free-running PIT ch0. Iron log line: `link 2560x1440 total 2720x1481 blanking 160x41` · `windows agree within 12 ppm` · `241503 kHz, 13 ppm from the 241500 kHz step`.
- IRON-PROVEN DCN 2.1 OFFSETS (all BASE_IDX 2, per-instance stride 0x80): `OTG_CONTROL` 0x1b41 · `OTG_STATUS` 0x1b49 · `OTG_STATUS_FRAME_COUNT` 0x1b4c (these three are the header-validation anchors — they matched `dcn_2_1_0_offset.h` exactly, which is what licenses trusting further offsets from that header). Also `OTG_H_TOTAL` 0x1b2a · `OTG_V_TOTAL` 0x1b2f · `OTG_H_TIMING_CNTL` 0x1b2e · `OTG_INTERLACE_CONTROL` 0x1b44. Totals are field `[14:0]`.
- TOTALS ENCODING: **both** `OTG_H_TOTAL` and `OTG_V_TOTAL` hold `total - 1`. `optc1_program_timing` has `/* CRTC_H_TOTAL = vesa.h_total - 1 */`, and on the line BEFORE the `OTG_V_TOTAL` write does `v_total = patched_crtc_timing.v_total - 1;` — the V write only LOOKS raw. This is a counter-wrap hardware convention every firmware obeys, not a driver choice. `apply_front_porch_workaround()` clamps `v_front_porch` only and never touches a total.
- BLANK REGISTERS CARRY NO -1: `active = OTG_H/V_BLANK_START_END START - END`, convention-free. Self-check: `h_total - h_active = 2720 - 2560 = 160` lands exactly on CVT-RB's mandated hblank; a wrong -1 gives 159 and matches no standard. The `blanking 160x41` boot log line IS that self-check, not decoration.
- dcn21 has **NO `OTG_H_TIMING_DIV_MODE` field** (zero hits in its `_sh_mask.h`) — only `DIV_BY2` bit0. The dcn10 code's DIV_MODE branch is for later parts.
- THE PIPE SCALER IS LIVE: firmware left an **800x600 GOP surface on a 2560x1440 link**. gnoboot reads fb_width from `Mode->Info->HorizontalResolution` AFTER `SetMode`, so boot_info is NOT stale — the firmware genuinely chose 800x600. Confirmed at 1.55.11 from the blank registers (link active reads 2560x1440).
- DSCL INSPECTION REGISTERS (for settling upscale-vs-centre, DPP per-instance stride 0x16B): `DSCL0_SCL_MODE` 0x0CEC (`DSCL_MODE[2:0]`, 6 = bypass) · `SCL_HORZ_FILTER_SCALE_RATIO` 0x0CF1 (ratio = raw / 2^24) · `RECOUT_SIZE` 0x0D03. DSCL sits UPSTREAM of OTG/DIG/PHY, so scaling cannot touch the link clock.
- AUDIO DTO MATH (`dce_audio.c` `get_azalia_clock_info_hdmi`): `audio_dto_module = actual_pixel_clock_100Hz`, `phase = 24 * 10000`. Sampling rate Fs NEVER enters the computation (settled at P3a).
- AUDIO DTO WRITE ORDER IS HARDWARE-ENFORCED: source/select (`DTO0_SOURCE_SEL` + `DTO_SEL`) MUST be written before MODULE and PHASE (AMD's own `dce_audio.c` warning); a violation fails **silently**. MODULE-before-PHASE merely matches AMD's observed order and is NOT documented as required.
- P3a STATE PROBE RESULTS (iron 1.55.8/1.55.9): link is HDMI on DIG1 at DIG_MODE=2; **DMCUB is not in the path**; the **AZ window is awake and correctly addressed** (proven by the index-writeback discriminator) so **no SMU PME mailbox is needed**; the audio DTOs are unprogrammed. `DP_DTO0_ENABLE`=0, `DP_VID_STREAM_ENABLE`=0.
- SSC IS ON DPREFCLK BUT NOT ON THE TMDS PIXEL CLOCK: DPREFCLK measured 598.875 MHz (0.1875% downspread), yet the pixel clock measured 241.502 — ABOVE the 241.500 nominal. A downspread pixel clock would read ~241.05.
- 241.5 MHz AT DIG_MODE=2 IS NOT A CONTRADICTION: that exceeds single-link *DVI's* 165 MHz ceiling, but this is DVI **signalling on an HDMI physical link** — 7.245 Gbps = 71% of HDMI 1.4's 10.2 Gbps ceiling. Consistent with `DP_DTO0_ENABLE`=0 and the absence of display audio (DVI signalling carries no audio and no infoframes).
- TIMEBASE: `pit_ch0_read()` in `apic.cyr` is a non-destructive latch read of PIT channel 0, which `pic_init()` leaves free-running as a **mode-2 rate generator, divisor 11932**. `pic_mask_pit()` masks IRQ0 at the 8259 but never touches the counter. Mode 2 decrements by ONE per tick and reloads after exactly `divisor` ticks — mode 3 would step by two and break the wrap math. **CPUID 0x15/0x16 return zeros on this exact part**, so the TSC route is closed.
- MEASUREMENT HYGIENE: the GPU probes run with **IF=0** (no `sti` anywhere earlier in the boot path — "Interrupts enabled" is ~2000 lines later), so no ISR/scheduler can perturb a window. SMI is the only contaminant, can only ADD time, and only biases when it lands ON a window endpoint — hence several short windows, keep the shortest. Compute from the RAW PIT tick count, never a rounded mHz refresh (a +/-0.5 mHz print quantum is ~8 ppm at 60 Hz).
- GATE ON CROSS-WINDOW AGREEMENT, NOT ON A SNAP TABLE: refuse if window spread > 200 ppm (iron measures ~17 ppm), accept a wide 5-600 MHz band. Every plausible fault lands far outside (a misread total ~1300 ppm, a missed PIT wrap ~3%) while a correct read lands within tens of ppm.
- ACR: when audio egress is attempted, use **HW auto-CTS** (`HDMI_ACR_AUTO_SEND` / `ACR_CONT`) — the hardware measures CTS off the real TMDS and real audio clock, so the derived pixel clock never enters the ACR path at all. Accuracy was never the constraint anyway: DTO module and ACR CTS derive from the SAME believed clock, so error is self-consistent; even 176 ppm = 0.3 cents (JND ~5 cents) and clears IEC 60958-3 Level II (+/-1000 ppm) by 5.7x.
- P4 ROOT CAUSE (`fb_console.cyr:88-91`): with quiet-boot ON the GOP leaves the scanout **tiled/DCC** while `fb_console` writes it **linear** — the swizzle bands the glyphs. It survived 2 pre-P1 attempts; P0's HUBP read exposing `DCSURF_*_TILING`/swizzle plus P1's linear re-point were the leverage that closed it (landed 1.55.28).
- P7's PROBLEM STATEMENT (durable even though the bite may have landed): blit#39 assumes **full-redraw-every-frame**, so a partial-update compositor (aethersafha) would see 2-frame-old content. The fix shape is `gpu_present(surface[, dirty])` + a ring-3 syscall that decouples RENDER from FLIP.
- P8 SCOPE: DCN hardware cursor plane lives in the **HUBP cursor registers** — an overlay so mouse motion does not recompose the frame. Display-safe, polish not a gate (software cursor works).

### From the shader (shipped 1.56.x) work
- S LANE COMPLETE: S0-S12 all iron-proven as of 2026-07-22. Arc opened 2026-07-22 immediately after 1.55.x (Thrust P / DISPLAY) closed with the CP-DMA 2D path and ring-3 band #84-#89 iron-proven on archaemenid. Version milestones inside the arc: 1.56.3 = execution divergence recorded; 1.56.4 = descriptor-array restore + S12 residency half; 1.56.5 = S12 batching half + pixel-identity oracle.
- S1 IRON FACT (durable): the SH compute registers are NOT GRBM-readable on this part (AMD Cezanne/Renoir gfx90c). Any plan that proposes reading back COMPUTE_PGM_RSRC1/RSRC2, USER_SGPR count, TGID enables, TIDIG_COMP_CNT, LDS_SIZE or COMPUTE_TMPRING_SIZE to verify what the code believes it wrote is not implementable via GRBM.
- S3 IRON FACT (all four arms conclusive, 2026-07-22, two burns): CP-DMA and shaders are in different coherence domains and BOTH coherence packets are required at EVERY engine-domain transition, in EITHER direction. CP-DMA neither snoops GL2 on read nor invalidates it on write. Measured negative evidence for the post-dispatch TC write-back: 4096-of-4096 dwords stale without it. This satisfied S12's stated precondition.
- gfx90c has NO shader-side L2 writeback: `buffer_wbl2` is UNSUPPORTED on this part (gfx90a has it, gfx90c does not). The CP-side post-dispatch TC write-back packet is the ONLY mechanism to make shader stores (which land in GL2) visible to DCN HUBP, which fetches DCHUB -> SDP -> data fabric and never traverses GL2.
- S12 MEASURED (iron, first burn 2026-07-22, BURN_SHADER_BATCH): batched one-submission mock frame = 107 us -> 60 us, 1.78x, pixel-identical to the same frame composited op-by-op. Residual analysis: ~87% fixed cost (60 us against ~8 us of real memory traffic). Calibration constant: 12 B/pixel, so a full-screen 1080p blend is ~25 MB - this path is MEMORY-BOUND, not ALU-bound.
- S4 IRON (first burn 2026-07-22, BURN_SHADER_PERM): the per-surface RGBX<->BGRX channel swap is ONE `v_perm_b32` VALU op (selector 0x03000102). Other proven selectors: 0x0c010c00 / 0x0c030c02 / 0x06040200 for unpack/repack identity, 0x0c030c03 for the alpha-byte-3 broadcast. Also confirmed on iron: `v_cvt_pk_u8_f32` CLAMPS negative inputs to 0.
- D1 IRON-CLOSED 2026-07-22 (code deleted 2026-07-23): HUBPRET channel crossbar does the whole-scanout RGBX<->BGRX swap in hardware, free, on the pipe agnos already owns. Fields in HUBPRET0_HUBPRET_CONTROL: CROSSBAR_SRC_ALPHA [17:16], SRC_Y_G [19:18], SRC_CB_B [21:20], SRC_CR_R [23:22]. The SURFACE_PIXEL_FORMAT value never changes (amdgpu implements ABGR8888 as ARGB8888 with the red/blue bars swapped).
- D2 IRON-CLOSED 2026-07-22 (code deleted 2026-07-23): agnos owns the MPC blender. MPCC_ALPHA_BLND_MODE [5:4] = 2 (GLOBAL_ALPHA) plus a sweep of MPCC_GLOBAL_ALPHA [23:16] under the OTG master update lock fades the whole screen toward the MPCC background colour and restores cleanly; underflow status stayed 0. This proved base + offset + field placement + latching + lock semantics in one shot.
- DCN 2.1 offsets confirmed 9-for-9 against agnos's iron-proven OTG/HUBP values, sourced from the generated `dcn_2_1_0_offset.h`: MPCC stride 0x1B, MPCC0_TOP_SEL @ 0x1271 (BASE_IDX 2), MPC_OUT0_MUX @ 0x1385, HUBPRET0_HUBPRET_CONTROL @ 0x066c (HUBP stride 0xDC), OPTC0 underflow @ 0x1aca bit 10.
- D0 read-only probe, all NINE constraints agreed on iron (six positive + three negative): MPCC0 TOP_SEL==0, OPP_ID==0, BOT_SEL==0xF, MPC_OUT0_MUX==0, MPCC0 STATUS BUSY=1/IDLE=0/DISABLED=0; MPCC1..5 all 0xF with IDLE=1; DOMAIN2/DOMAIN3 power-GATED; OPTC0 underflow status 0.
- Grid dispatch primitives landed and iron-proven at S5: RSRC2 = 0x190 enables TGID_X/TGID_Y; the workgroup ids arrive in `s8` (tgid_x) and `s9` (tgid_y), the SGPRs immediately after the user SGPRs. A `gpu_shader_run` sibling taking rsrc2 + workgroup size + grid as parameters now exists; `gpu_matmul_run` (`gpu.cyr:1988`, 68-dword envelope) was left byte-identical so the three ML proof paths did not move. SET_SH_REG_N8/_N10 headers minted from the formula at `gpu_regs.cyr:948`. 64-bit VGPR-pair addressing retained (SADDR form is a later optimisation).
- EXEC discipline on this raw non-HWS HQD path (S6, iron): `s_mov_b64 exec,-1` must come FIRST because the SPI hands the wave exec=0; the bounds guard is then `v_cmp_lt_u32_e64` + `s_and_b64 exec`. Shipped and iron-green, but at w=200 (not the planned w=1917) and with NO unguarded control arm.
- Syscall band as it stands: #82 gpu_matmul (gated by `gpu_matmul_ok`), #84 present, #85 clear, #86 seed, #72, #89 gpu_caps (carries a 'shader compositing available' flag bit); #90 `gpu_readback_shm` and #91 `gpu_blit_bb` RESERVED with declared shapes; #92 `gpu_shader_op(desc_uva, len)` is the single shader seam, taking an ARRAY of ops `{op, src_id, mask_id, wh, dstxy, alpha, flags}` copied in via `proc_copy_from_user`, modelled on `gpu_blit_shm_sys` (`syscall.cyr:774`). No MC address crosses the ring-3 boundary; it rejects rather than clips. Ring-3 test binary is `/bin/gpublend`.
- Renoir/Cezanne gives exactly 4 BLENDABLE PLANES total - bounded by `num_timing_generator = 4`, NOT by the 6 MPCC instances - and they are the same 4 pipes a second monitor would want. A compositor with twenty translucent windows cannot map onto four planes. MPC blending is therefore a fast path for a few large surfaces only (wallpaper, one always-on translucent panel, cursor, one fullscreen window) and is complementary to the shader path, never a substitute.
- Shader slot layout: `GPU_SHADER_SUBOFF = 0x14000` was a single slot rewritten in place every dispatch (which is why the pre-dispatch I$ invalidate exists). S12 landed multi-slot residency: each kernel gets a 256-byte-aligned resident slot, and `COMPUTE_PGM_LO = mc>>8` must stay exact.
- S9 glyph expand structural win (iron): with the transparent background expressed as an EXEC mask the destination is NEVER loaded - 4 VALU + 1 store, ZERO loads - versus ~128 bit-tests + ~40 writes per glyph per frame on the CPU path. Consumes `kashi_glyph_row` (kashi VGA 8x16); touches nothing inside kashi.
- `global_load_ubyte` assembles on gfx90c and is now in the tree (S10 coverage-mask path). The byte->(c,c) broadcast is one `v_perm_b32`.
- Toolchain reality: `scripts/gfx9-asm.sh` assembles shaders from `.s` via `llvm-mc -mcpu=gfx90c` and harvests RSRC1/RSRC2 - better than hand-typed hex, and it is what actually shipped.
- Comment rot to distrust in the GPU tree, verified: several comments say 'the 39-dword ring program' but the C2f envelope is 50 dwords and `gpu_matmul_run` is 68 - COUNT `gpu_ring_put` calls, do not trust the comments. `gpu_regs.cyr:1028` still says `v_fma_f64` where the shipped kernel uses separate `v_mul_f64` + `v_add_f64`.

### From the modeset / HDMI-audio investigation work
- A4 STATUS (as of 1.55.26, 2026-07-20): HDMI audio on archaemenid (AMD Cezanne/Renoir, DCN 2.1, XB323U over HDMI) is still SILENT. Everything measurable is green: clock alive, FIFO drains, real PCM at both AFMT CRC taps, ACR alive, AVMUTE clear, channel status normal, 40-register file byte-matched to amdgpu-playing, sink amp armed. A0-A3 DONE and iron-proven; A4 open.
- THE 0x5Dxx/0x5Exx PHY/RDPCS MMIO DOMAIN IS READ-SAFE. `gop-dvi-dump.py` Group C completed without hanging (2026-07-20, capture `agnosticos/docs/development/prior-art/dcn-gop-dvi-readonly-0720.txt`). Every prior plan treated this domain as a hang risk to be dodged; it is not. agnos may read its own PHY block from the kernel.
- THE LIVE LINK IS DIG1 IN DVI MODE at boot. GOP-DVI read: `DIG1_DIG_BE_CNTL = 0x10020200` (DIG_MODE=2=DVI, DIG_ENABLE=1); DIG0 is off (mode 1, disabled). Confirms agnos inherits DIG1-DVI and that agnos's ATOM `#4 digid=1` is the correct encoder index.
- THE ONLY DIG_BE DELTA BETWEEN GOP-DVI AND amdgpu-HDMI-PLAYING IS DIG_MODE (bit16). GOP-DVI `0x10020200` vs amdgpu-HDMI-playing `0x10030200`; `FE_SOURCE_SELECT=0x2` in both. agnos already writes `0x10030200` (and `DIG_FE_CNTL 0x01000100`, STEREOSYNC_GATE bit8) and gets no audio.
- `HDMI_CONTROL` on agnos reads `0x10019`, byte-matching the amdgpu known-good. CAUTION: bit3 (`HDMI_NO_EXTRA_NULL_PACKET_FILLED`) reads 1 in the 0716 amdgpu capture and 0 in a fresh 0720 amdgpu capture — it varies between two AUDIBLY-PLAYING captures, so it cannot gate audio, and single-snapshot register comparisons must not be over-read.
- FULL 40-REGISTER agnos-vs-amdgpu DIFF (agnos = `audio_re_11`, amdgpu = `dcn-audio-known-good-full-0716`): 35 exact matches, 5 differences, ALL accounted for — `HDMI_ACR_STATUS_0` (read-only HW-measured CTS, 241502 vs 241500 = a measurement not a write), `HDMI_ACR_48_0/44_0/32_0` (agnos 0 vs amdgpu `0x3af5c000`), and `AFMT_GENERIC_0` (deliberate RGB-vs-YCbCr AVI infoframe).
- REGISTER-OFFSET AUDIT: 105/105 audio-block offsets in `gpu_regs.cyr` verified correct against canonical Linux v6.6 headers `dcn_2_1_0_offset.h` + `dce_11_0_d.h`, 0 mismatch, BASE_IDX correct throughout (DIG/AFMT/HDMI/AZ/DMCUB = 2; DCCG audio-DTO and pixel-rate = 1). Method + 105-row table + header hashes: `agnosticos/docs/development/prior-art/dcn-audio-register-offset-audit-0720.md`.
- VERIFIED REGISTER OFFSETS (DCN 2.1, BASE_IDX 2 unless noted): `DIG_FE_CNTL 0x2068`, `DIG_BE_CNTL 0x20af`, `DIG_BE_EN_CNTL 0x20b0`, `AFMT_STATUS 0x20a9`, `HDMI_AUDIO_PACKET_CONTROL 0x2073`, `HDMI_ACR_PACKET_CONTROL 0x2074`, `HDMI_ACR_STATUS_0 0x209C`, `HDMI_ACR_48_0 0x209A`, `HDMI_ACR_44_0 0x2098`, `HDMI_ACR_32_0 0x2096`; DCCG audio-DTO block `0xab-0xaf` and `DP_DTO_MODULO 0x82` at BASE_IDX 1; `DCCG_GTC_CURRENT 0x0063` BASE_IDX 1; `DMCUB_INBOX1_WPTR` abs `0x6740`.
- MEASURED CLOCKS: agnos's real pixel clock is 241503 kHz. `DP_DTO_MODULO 0x82` parses to DPREFCLK 598.875 MHz. `HDMI_ACR_STATUS_0` reads `0x3af5e000`/`0x3af5f000`/`0x3af60000` = HW-measured CTS 241502/241503/241504, drifting +/-1-2 LSB boot to boot, while dead DIGs read `0x00000000`.
- THE AUDIO CLOCK IS ALIVE AND IN THE MEASUREMENT LOOP (burn 11, 1.55.24, `audio_re_11.txt`). Programming `HDMI_ACR_N_48` from 6144 to 12288 moved the measured CTS `241502 -> 483006 -> 241502`, exactly 2x with N. `HDMI_ACR_48_0/44_0/32_0` all read 0 alongside, so `HDMI_ACR_STATUS_0` is a genuine hardware measurement and not an echo of a write.
- CTS ARITHMETIC (needed to read the instrument honestly): `CTS = f_TMDS*N/(128*Fs)`; at N=6144 / Fs=48 kHz this reduces to exactly `f_pixel` in kHz, so a single unmodified CTS read cannot distinguish 'measured against the audio clock' from 'counting the pixel clock'. The N-doubling test above is what separates them.
- `DCCG_AUDIO_DTO0_MODULE` DOES NOT match amdgpu: iron reads `0x24d9b2`, amdgpu's constant is `0x24d998` — 26 counts / ~10.8 ppm, because agnos derives MODULE from the live pixel clock instead of copying amdgpu's literal. `DTO0_PHASE` (`0x3a980` = 240000, 'generate 24.000 MHz') and `DCCG_AUDIO_DTO_SOURCE` (`0`) DO match exactly, on agnos and on the audibly-playing amdgpu link.
- CONTENT REACHES THE LAST OBSERVABLE STAGE. The calibrated tap-identity experiment established that digital silence reads exactly `0x000000` at BOTH AFMT CRC taps. Burn 11 read `tap0=249f2f`, `tap1=b9b93c` — both content-bearing. Standing caveat: the CRC taps are AMPLITUDE-BLIND below about -48 dB, so non-zero does not prove audible level.
- THE READ SIDE DRAINS — the underrun/FIFO hypothesis is dead on data in hand. `AFMT_AUDIO_CRC_DONE` asserts only after `CRC_COUNT` (2048) samples physically traverse the AFMT. Pre-feed both taps read 'did not complete'; post-feed both complete with distinct non-zero values.
- THE SINK'S AMP IS ARMED AND DRIVEN BY agnos's STREAM. Evidence: agnos produces a shutdown release pop on reboot (first heard `audio_re_5` / 1.55.20) where Linux does not, because Linux tears the audio path down before power-off and agnos cuts power with the amp still energised. Exposed side-bite: agnos has NO orderly audio teardown.
- THE FEED IS EXONERATED. `agnosticos/scripts/agnos-hdmi-linux.py`, a from-scratch Linux userspace driver reproducing agnos's ENTIRE feed verbatim from `hda.cyr` (HDA controller + codec verbs + DMA + tone) and riding amdgpu's cold modeset, PLAYED AUDIBLE SOUND. Instance-1's ring genuinely carries the -0.8 dBFS triangle. A morph test replaying agnos's `DIG_MODE 2->3` flip on amdgpu's committed pipe kept the tone playing, so the DIG-leaf flip is innocent too.
- SOVEREIGN ATOM INTERPRETER IS IN HAND AND IRON-PROVEN BIT-CORRECT (1.55.23). `gpu_vbios_acquire()` in `gpu.cyr` parses the ACPI VFCT table, verifies VendorID=0x1002, copies the ATOM image to a 2 MB page, checks the `55aa` signature, sets `gpu_vbios_va/len` (compiled unconditionally, outside `#ifdef HDMI_AUDIO_DUMP`). `kernel/core/atom.cyr` (~600 lines) is a faithful Cyrius port of `scripts/atom-interp.py` (1461 lines, verbatim port of amdgpu `atom.c`: all 41 `atom_op_*`, 127-entry `opcode_table[]`, all 8 address spaces + `ATOM_WS_*` specials + IIO). The iron DRY trace = encoder #4 five writes + transmitter #76 21 reads / 17 writes / 5 delays, matching the Python oracle EXACTLY, with no amdgpu present. This is the P6 cold-modeset foundation.
- VBIOS MAP (archaemenid, 54 KB, dumped via `sudo cat /sys/kernel/debug/dri/1/amdgpu_vbios`, saved as `vbios.rom`): atomfirmware layout, MasterCommandTable @0x93fa. Transmitter control = command-table index 76, crev `v1_6`, 262 B bytecode @0xb162. `DIGxEncoderControl` = index 4, `v1_5`, 782 B @0xae4e. Also present: `SetPixelClock` #12 v1_7, `EnableDispPowerGating` #13. The tables branch on `SWITCH` and use `SETPORT` within ~9 bytes = parameterized control-flow bytecode, NOT a linear replayable sequence.
- ATOM INTERPRETER PARAMS for transmitter #76: `symclk=24150`, `lanenum=4`, `digmode=HDMI`, `action=ENABLE` are exact. `phyid` was WRONG at 0 — the live path is PHY B / phyid=1 (see hazards). `digfe_sel` (0x02?) and `hpdsel` (0) remain unconfirmed on iron.
- ATOM INTERPRETER AUDIT (2026-07-18): 10 findings, 1 confirmed — `atom_op_div32` used Cyrius's SIGNED `/` on a 64-bit dividend that goes negative when `divmul1`'s MSB is set, diverging from the oracle's unsigned divide. FIXED with bit-by-bit unsigned division. DIV32 is not used by the #76/#4 tables so A4 was unaffected either way.
- DMCUB IS DORMANT AT BOOT (T2 read-only burn, `audio_re_8`, 2026-07-18): `CC_DC_PIPE_DIS=0x10000` (block present), `DMCUB_CNTL=0x20000` (SOFT_RESET set, ENABLE clear -> held in reset, not running), `SCRATCH0=0` (no firmware), `INBOX1 base/size/wptr/rptr all 0` (no ring). The display is up yet the DMCUB never ran, therefore the GOP brought the transmitter up via HOST-ATOM. DMCUB is an OS-driver-loaded thing; agnos's boot-inherited world is host-ATOM.
- `symclk_se` DOES NOT EXIST on DCN 2.1 — it was added in 2024 for dcn35/dcn401. Source-verified 2026-07-17 against the real amdgpu headers.
- amdgpu's DMCUB inbox anatomy (from the 2026-07-17 capture, retained as mechanism reference only): `DMCUB_INBOX1_WPTR` (abs 0x6740) advances monotonically in +0x40 (64-byte) steps, 106 commands, during a modeset.

### From the HDMI-audio physical model work
- HDMI PHYSICAL MODEL (durable, unique to this doc — nowhere else in agnos/docs): an HDMI link is a DVI link that steals the blanking. TMDS is always in one of three periods — Video Data (active pixels, 8b/10b), Control (filler), or Data Island (DIP). Audio + aux packets ride ONLY in horizontal/vertical blanking, guard-banded, TERC4-coded (NOT 8b/10b), BCH-protected. Consequence: video working over HDMI proves nothing about audio, because DVI and HDMI video are bit-identical.
- There is no 'I am HDMI' bit on the wire. DVI vs HDMI is BEHAVIORAL: the source carves data islands into blanking and fills them; the sink infers 'HDMI' by receiving valid data islands (above all the AVI InfoFrame). No islands -> sink stays in DVI receive mode with nowhere to route audio. The source is permitted to send islands because the sink's EDID carries the HDMI VSDB (IEEE OUI 0x000C03). The XB323U HAS the VSDB, so that gate was never agnos's blocker.
- Packet set required for stereo 48 kHz L-PCM, all sustained on cadence in the DIP: (a) Audio Sample Packet type 0x02 — the PCM, ~12,000 non-empty stereo ASPs/sec; `sample_present` selects which of 4 subpackets carry data; the B/block-start bit marks IEC-60958 channel-status boundaries; stereo = Layout 0. (b) Audio Clock Regeneration type 0x01 — sink regenerates 128*fs from TMDS via N/CTS: 128*fs = f_TMDS*(N/CTS); 48 kHz -> N=6144; at 241.5 MHz pixel clock -> CTS=241500; sent >=1/field. (c) Audio InfoFrame type 0x84 — CT/CC/CA metadata, >=1 per 2 fields; A SINK RECEIVING ASPs WITH NO AIF MUST MUTE; on DCN the AIF content is HW-generated from the Azalia CHANNEL_SPEAKER and the source only sets the SEND bit. (d) General Control Packet type 0x03 — carries AVMUTE; many sinks unmute only on an AVMUTE SET->CLEAR EDGE, not on a steady-cleared state (the vc4_hdmi upstream fix).
- The irreducible HDMI-audio bring-up ORDER, convergent across discrete TX chips (ADV7511 / IT66121 / SiI9022), vc4, i915, and pre-DMUB AMD DCE — and always executed as part of a MODESET, never as a live poke: (a) bring the port up and let TMDS settle / link-train FIRST (audio is downstream of a freshly-active link); (b) hold the audio path in reset while programming clock / N-CTS / format / channel-status / InfoFrame, then release on a clean EDGE; (c) SW writes N, HW derives CTS; (d) send AVI + Audio InfoFrame continuously; (e) schedule ASPs and CLEAR AVMUTE LAST (SET during setup -> CLEAR when ready = the edge the sink waits for). Discrete chips hide this behind one HDMI-mode bit that triggers an internal PHY transition + output bounce; AMD exposes the same transition as the DIG_ENABLE output-enable edge, the MMIO equivalent of ATOM TransmitterControl(ENABLE, HDMI).
- IRON PROOF, 2026-07-14 green-screen (the doc's single most load-bearing positive result, found nowhere else in the docs): when agnos briefly wrote amdgpu's YCbCr AVI bytes, the panel came up GREEN/PINK. A sink can only mis-decode RGB-as-YCbCr if it is receiving, parsing, and HONORING agnos's AVI InfoFrame. Therefore DIG_MODE=3 took, the DIP engine runs, the AVI egresses, and the sink treats the link as HDMI.
- AVI InfoFrames and Audio Sample Packets come from DIFFERENT generators: AVI = the generic-packet SEND engine (CPU writes payload + sets SEND bit); ASPs = the AFMT draining its FIFO, gated on SAMPLE_SEND + ACR + DIP scheduling. 'AVI egresses' therefore does NOT prove 'ASPs egress'. This is why the green-screen result, while dispositive about HDMI mode, is silent about audio.
- INSTRUMENT LIMITS of AFMT_AUDIO_CRC (standing caution): pre-1.55.19, tap1 read frozen at 0xd97638 across EVERY probe — initial enable and sweep profiles 14 and 15 alike — because it was reading static IEC-60958 framing, not audio. The CRC is also AMPLITUDE-BLIND: the 1.55.17 -48 dB sign-folded packing bug lit it identically to a correct tone. Never adjudicate a burn on the CRC alone.
- DCN 2.1's audio block is pure MMIO (`enc1_se_*`, no `dc_dmub_srv` anywhere in the audio path). DMUB owns PSR / ABM / idle, not AFMT. Pre-DMUB AMD (DCE8/10/11, GCN, no display microcontroller) does HDMI audio 100% via REG_UPDATE. The ftrace observation of 'zero DIG/AFMT writes during playback or modeset' means the block is configured ONCE at modeset and then emits islands autonomously per-frame — steady-state autonomy, NOT a firmware hand-off.
- The DIG_ENABLE back-end toggle is IRON-PROVEN RECOVERABLE — burns 14 and 15 both booted through it. (Contrast with ATOM transmitter table #76, which blanks the pipe non-recoverably.) This makes DIG_ENABLE cycling a safe primitive even though it is not the fix.
- agnos's implemented source-side program, itemised (byte-identical to the amdgpu-audibly-playing DIG1 audio set except deliberate/benign diffs — RGB-vs-YCbCr AVI, ~11 ppm CTS, the FIFO-overflow symptom): DIG_MODE=3, HDMI_CONTROL keepout + packet-gen, DCCG audio DTO (SEL=0), the AZ endpoint (clock-ungate -> CHANNEL_SPEAKER + HDMI_CONNECTION -> AUDIO_ENABLED -> slot map -> the `dce_aud_az_configure` descriptors), AFMT clock, AVI + Audio InfoFrame SEND, ACR N=6144 / AUTO_SEND, IEC-60958 channel status, SAMPLE_SEND.
- Drain-before-feed implementation as shipped at 1.55.19 (keep the mechanism even though it was not the answer): `hda_hdmi_feed_stop()` / `hda_hdmi_feed_start()` in hda.cyr clear/set SD_RUN on HDA instance 1 (the HDMI stream is tag 3). `gpu_hdmi_audio_enable` calls `_stop` at the top so all encoder-audio setup runs against a quiesced FIFO, then arms the drain on an empty FIFO in the order overflow-ack -> AVMUTE-clear -> SAMPLE_SEND 0->1. `main.cyr` calls `_start` as the TRUE TERMINAL OP, after `hda_hdmi_bind_single` — because bind-single re-issues SET_STREAM_FMT and would re-glitch the FIFO phase if the feed were already running. This mirrors ALSA: prepare() configures the drain, trigger(START) starts the feed last.
- FIFO-phase mechanism (reference model, still the correct description of the pre-1.55.19 defect): `hda_stream_arm` set SD_RUN on the HDMI stream seconds before `gpu_hdmi_audio_enable` opened the AFMT drain, so the GPU HDMI codec free-ran PCM into the AFMT FIFO with the read side closed; the write pointer lapped a never-drained FIFO (AFMT_STATUS bit24 AUDIO_FIFO_OVERFLOW latched early) and when SAMPLE_SEND finally opened, the read pointer was permanently out of phase and the IEC-60958 formatter clocked out null cells.

---

## ⛔ Falsified — do not re-open
Each was tested and killed. Iron burns block the operator's only dev machine; re-opening one of these wastes a burn. This is the highest-value section in the file.

### From the compute (shipped 1.54.x) work
- **"The BIOS/PSP leaves usable CP/MEC/RLC ucode resident, so agnos can skip the firmware load" — KILLED at C0 (iron, agnos 1.54.1, 2026-07-11).** Evidence: `rlc=0x0`, `me=0x15000000`, `mec=0x50000000` — RLC off, CP and MEC both halted, no ucode. Adversarially confirmed against primary AMD sources. This was Case B, and it forced C1 to be "drive the PSP GPCOM firmware-load handshake." Do NOT spend a burn re-probing for resident ucode.
- **"Compute needs gfx9 GPUVM per-VMID multi-level page tables (PTE/PDE + TLB flush)" — KILLED at C2a (iron, agnos 1.54.8).** Evidence: the read-only GMC/GPUVM probe found **GART ABSENT**; the FB carveout sits at MC `[0xF400000000,+3GB)`. Zero-page-table addressing through the BIOS FB aperture with `VM_CONTEXT0` disabled was then proven to work end-to-end at C2d (`fault=0` on the first PM4 fetch). Building gfx9 page tables for compute scratch on this part is dead work AND re-introduces the VM-fault-storm CPU wedge the current design excludes.
- **"The BAR2 posted doorbell advances the queue wptr" — KILLED at C2d (iron, agnos 1.54.12).** Evidence: the posted BAR2 doorbell write did NOT advance the wptr; the **register-wptr submit works** and is what every subsequent bite uses. (Consistent with the standing finding that agnos's doorbell aperture-enable is incomplete on archaemenid, `cap=0` — an agnos gap, not a hardware limit.) Do not re-attempt doorbell submit as a "cleanup"; it costs a burn and has already failed.
- **"`ACQUIRE_MEM` with `GPU_CP_COHER_CNTL_TCWB` is write-BACK, not invalidate" — FALSE; corrected 2026-07-22 and this is the highest-cost doc error in the arc.** `0x00840000` carries `TC_ACTION_ENA` (1<<23) = L2 **INVALIDATE** as well as `TC_WB_ACTION_ENA` (1<<18). Evidence: RADV IB decode on this same Cezanne — `mabda .../radv-triangle.ib.txt:12-27` (field mapping) and `:1649-1656` (`CACHE_FLUSH_AND_INV_TS_EVENT` with `{TC_WB=1, TC=1}`). Consequence on record: the wrong claim is the **root cause of plan-S3 arm D's design**, which primed L2 with a dispatch whose own trailing packet then invalidated the lines it had just populated — making **both sub-arms unfalsifiable on the 1.56.4 burn**. The author had reasoned about exactly that hazard one comment earlier and reintroduced it four lines later because the doc said the packet could not invalidate.
- **"The gfx90c ISA / the shader itself is wrong" — KILLED across 3 burns at C2f (agnos 1.54.16).** The hand-assembled kernel was correct the whole time; the actual defect was the **WPTR LO-before-HI submit-order** requirement (the CP latches on the HI write). Blaming the ISA before byte-confirming the submit sequence cost two burns.
- **"You can CPU-pre-seed a buffer (UC) and use the read-back as the oracle for whether the shader stored" — KILLED at C2g-1 burn 2 (agnos 1.54.18).** Evidence: the CPU read back only a **stale-L2 ghost of the previous C2f value**, so the test could not distinguish zero-waves from a wrong store address; 1.54.19 had to be spent on a fresh-slot isolation diagnostic. Always dispatch into a **fresh, never-CPU-touched slot**.
- **"Display audio might not be GPU-gated" — settled NEGATIVE earlier, 1.53.5 burn 2 dispositive:** display audio egress IS gated on owning the GPU display pipe, which is why P3/A4 sits inside the GPU work at all.

### From the display (shipped 1.55.x) work
- "The display is on DP output 2." KILLED at P3a (iron 1.55.8): `DP_DTO0_ENABLE`=0 and `DP_VID_STREAM_ENABLE`=0 ⇒ not DP-SST; the live encoder is DIG1 in DIG_MODE=2. The physical cable is HDMI (operator ground truth). This wrong table entry cost a detour. Corollary killed: the non-goal line "HDMI-first audio (DP first; HDMI ACR/CTS is a P3 follow-on)" — there is NO DP output on this part, HDMI audio was the whole audio effort.
- "Snap the measured refresh to a standard-rate table." BUILT at 1.55.10, **IRON FALSIFIED**, deleted at 1.55.11 after a 16-agent adversarial verification. The table snapped to **59.94 Hz — a television rate on a PC monitor** — converting a 7.5 ppm measurement into a **176 ppm** answer. Refresh is NOT a round number on PC modes: VESA CVT quantises the PIXEL CLOCK to a 0.25 MHz step (241.6992 floor→ 241.50); 59.9506 Hz is the LEFTOVER. Round-refresh is a CEA/TV property. Nothing should ever "correct" 59.9506 toward 60.000.
- "Snap the pixel clock to the 0.25 MHz CVT grid instead — a grid is safer than a table." KILLED analytically on this link's own data: the **WRONG** totals decode (raw 2719x1480) lands **+0.08 ppm** from a grid step while the **CORRECT** decode lands **+7.5 ppm** — the bug fits the grid **75x better than the truth**. A grid cannot tell correct from plausible; it launders a register-decode bug into a confident exact number. Also CVT-RBv2 uses a 0.001 MHz step (modern VRR panels are off-grid), legacy DMT 25.175/28.322 are off-grid, and a /1.001 grid sits -27.7 ppm from this clock ⇒ ambiguous. **DO NOT SNAP AT ALL — the measured value IS the answer.**
- "The 500 ppm snap tolerance gates the derivation." KILLED: it was **dead code** across [59.94, 60] — the candidates are ~1000 ppm apart and the gate sat at the half-spacing, so something ALWAYS matched. A gate must be placed on an axis where it can actually fire (cross-window agreement), not on the candidate axis.
- "Derive timing / pixel clock / vblank period from `fb_width()`/`fb_height()`." KILLED — this was 1.55.10's guard bug. The firmware left an 800x600 GOP surface on a 2560x1440 link and the DCN pipe scaler is LIVE; the guard passed only by accident because 2720 > 800. `fb_width/fb_height` are the SURFACE plane, not the RASTER plane.
- "Either OTG total identifies the mode on its own." KILLED by brute-force scan: **2720** also fits 2560x1080 and 2560x1600 at every rate (CVT-RB's fixed 160 hblank pins only the active WIDTH), and **1481** also fits 1920x1440@60 and 3440x1440@60. Only the (h_total, v_total) PAIR is unique. Always key on the pair.
- "The DVI→HDMI `DIG_MODE` 2→3 flip rewrites the encoder and is a high-risk burn." KILLED as overstated at P3b-ii (built 1.55.12, gated on `HDA_HDMI`): every `HDMI_*` register is **inert while `DIG_MODE==2`**, so the entire HDMI config is staged at zero risk and the flip is a one-field **RMW** commit of an already-verified context. `dcn10_link_encoder_setup` is a single `REG_UPDATE` — no PHY/PLL/OTG touched, so it cannot move the clock or the timing.
- "DMCUB is in the path / gates lit-pipe flips and audio." KILLED read-only at P3a (iron 1.55.8): DMCUB is not in the path.
- "The AZ (Azalia) window is asleep and needs an SMU PME mailbox poke." KILLED at P3a: the index-writeback discriminator proves the AZ window is awake and correctly addressed. No SMU mailbox is needed.
- "Read the pixel clock out of a register." KILLED: the DCCG register holding the audio DTO MODULE is only populated on a **DP-driven** pipe — this link is HDMI so it reads **0**. amdgpu has no PHY-PLL read-back on the HDMI path (`get_pixel_clk_frequency_100hz` reads the DP DTO — the register that reads 0 here); dividers come from ATOM BIOS tables. Divider math returns the un-spread CENTRE while frame-counting returns the AVERAGE — and the average is what the audio DTO wants. **The measurement is more correct than the register.**
- "The DCN audio silence is somewhere in the audio register programming / the feed / the codec / the sample magnitude / the DCN write sequence." **REGISTER CLASS EXHAUSTED at 1.55.23** (work spanning 1.55.8-1.55.21): every AFMT/AZ/DIG/audio-clock register is byte-matched to an amdgpu-PLAYING capture, and a from-scratch **Linux userspace repro of agnos's own feed plays audible sound** — feed, codec, magnitude and DCN sequence are ALL EXONERATED. It is still silent on agnos. The residual is the **firmware HDMI transmitter bring-up** the GOP ran as DVI (source-verified 2026-07-17). Do not re-audit audio registers.
- "agnos has no ATOM interpreter, so the transmitter bring-up must be capture-and-replay of amdgpu's ATOM writes." SUPERSEDED at 1.55.23 — agnos HAS a sovereign ~700-line Cyrius ATOM BIOS interpreter (`kernel/core/atom.cyr`) that runs the vendor's own VBIOS transmitter/encoder bytecode directly; A2/A3 are bit-correct on iron. The whole P5a-capture / P5b-derive framing is dead.
- "Gate anything at DVI's 165 MHz ceiling." KILLED — this link legitimately runs 241.5 MHz at DIG_MODE=2 (DVI signalling on an HDMI physical link). A 165 MHz gate would falsely refuse a correct read.
- "SDMA is the right primitive for 2D acceleration (P9), not the GFX/compute ring." KILLED by the delivered 1.55.x close: 2D landed via **CP-DMA on the compute ring**, not SDMA. Related standing finding: on archaemenid both MEC and SDMA **doorbells fail (cap=0)** — an agnos aperture-enable gap, not a hardware limit — so register-wptr submit is the working path.
- "Use the PIT in mode 3 as the measurement timebase." Ruled out by construction: mode 3 steps the counter by TWO per tick and breaks the wrap math. Mode 2 (what `pic_init()` already leaves running, divisor 11932) decrements by one and reloads after exactly `divisor` ticks.

### From the shader (shipped 1.56.x) work
- KILLED (S3, iron 2026-07-22, two burns): the hope that the GL2/CP-DMA/scanout domains might be incidentally coherent, or that the post-dispatch TC write-back might be optional. All four arms ran conclusive - 4096-of-4096 dwords read STALE without the write-back. CP-DMA neither snoops GL2 on read nor invalidates it on write, so both coherence packets are mandatory at every engine-domain transition in BOTH directions. Do not re-open 'can we drop a cache op here'.
- KILLED (S12, iron 2026-07-22, first burn): 'the per-dispatch fence/submission overhead is the throughput wall.' Batching to one submission with a single CS_PARTIAL_FLUSH + one write-back + one fence gave only 1.78x (107 us -> 60 us) and the frame remains ~87% fixed cost - 60 us against ~8 us of real memory traffic. The remaining wall is the SIX per-dispatch whole-L2 invalidates and flushes, not the fence.
- KILLED (S1, iron): the plan's premise that a read-only compute-state probe could decode RSRC1/RSRC2 fields, USER_SGPR count, TGID enables, TIDIG_COMP_CNT, LDS_SIZE and especially `COMPUTE_TMPRING_SIZE` to check for a stale scratch base. The SH compute registers are not GRBM-readable on this part; that oracle cannot be built this way. S1 and D0 shipped as reports, not gates.
- KILLED (D1 + S4, iron 2026-07-22): 'RGBX<->BGRX channel permutation is an unhandled case, not just a slow one' - the framing this arc opened with. It is handled twice over: the whole-scanout case is FREE in the HUBPRET crossbar (two fields, one register, no second plane, no bandwidth change), and the per-surface composite case is ONE `v_perm_b32` (selector 0x03000102).
- KILLED (D-7, settled 2026-07-23): rebuilding the D lane as a permanent operator-triggered re-runnable ring-3 harness. D1 and D2 both CLOSED on iron across the five burns of 2026-07-22, so the lane has no remaining customer - the tell is that with the lane gone `gpu_otg_lock` and `gpu_dcn_write_committed` have ZERO callers. The rebuild was made on the strength of the (stale) ladder and iron log still listing D1/D2 as outstanding. `DCN_DLANE` is retired; code and flag deleted 2026-07-23.
- KILLED (D-lane post-mortem, five burns 2026-07-22): the assumption that those burns were spent on register work. FOUR OF FIVE were lost to the HARNESS: (a) a hold on a bounded vblank poll that collapsed a 4 s display window to ~50 ms; (b) TWO burns whose stimulus was an ACHROMATIC console, where any colour permutation is the identity and the test can never fail; (c) a register decoded by eye. D2 only closed once the test was made maximally loud - alpha driven to zero against a deliberately-set BLUE background. D1 only closed with an A/B/C/D/E phase burn putting the HUBPRET crossbar and SURFACE_PIXEL_FORMAT in ONE boot.
- KILLED (D-1 evaluation): vendoring a SPIR-V compiler in-kernel. It was actually built and is provably freestanding-clean (builds and runs standalone with `stdlib = []`, `--agnos` succeeds, byte-identical ISA) - and it still buys nothing: +4,641 LOC (+8.5%), ~80 KB stack per compile, pulls f32/f64 intrinsics into kernel text that is deliberately integer-only, and there is NO SPIR-V producer anywhere in the ecosystem, so it would mean shipping SPIR-V blobs instead of ISA blobs. Host-side offline emitter is the settled path.
- KILLED (S8 / D-3, reverted at 1.56.4): one syscall number per shader op. It was actually SHIPPED as #92 blend + #93 coverage + #94 glyph - verbatim the column D-3 had already rejected - and then restored to the single #92 descriptor-array ABI. Reasons the array wins stand: only four dispatcher args but a coverage blit needs five operands; #90/#91 are already reserved; four numbers = four separate cyrius asks against a ticket built to end exactly that pattern; and the array makes S12 batching an implementation change rather than an ABI break.
- KILLED (the *SCANOUT_REGDUMP* incident): 'cmp of the two binaries proves the #ifdef flag landed.' `cmp` proves the binaries DIFFER, not that the difference is the code under test. This tree does not run DCE by default, so a `kprintln` marker sitting in an always-compiled function is present in EVERY binary and verifies nothing - which is exactly how `*SCANOUT_REGDUMP*` shipped matching on the string "HUBP regdump begin". The verification must be a `verify_marker` row in `burn-prep.sh` naming a string INSIDE the flag's own `#ifdef`, PLUS the negative check that the string is ABSENT from a bare build.
- KILLED (by reasoning, at S6): `s_cbranch_execz` alone as the right-edge bounds guard. It only skips when ALL 64 lanes are out of bounds; the 1-63 straggler lanes are precisely the ones that store past the right edge. Caveat for the record: this was never proven with the planned negative arm - S6 shipped at w=200 with no unguarded control - so 'the guard stopped the overrun' is INFERRED, not measured.
- KILLED (D-2 in practice, though never ratified): the plan's two-way choice between exact floor `(x + 1 + (x>>8)) >> 8` and the `a256` approximation. S2 shipped as f32 PREMULTIPLIED - a third option D-2 never listed. The row remains formally unratified and the shipped semantics do not match either recommended branch.

### From the modeset / HDMI-audio investigation work
- FALSIFIED — 'THE SOVEREIGN COLD HDMI MODESET / TRANSMITTER BRING-UP IS THE MISSING PIECE.' Killed 2026-07-20 by matched read-only captures: the PHY / RDPCS block is BYTE-IDENTICAL between agnos-inherited-DVI and amdgpu-HDMI-playing. Every `RDPCSTX0/1`, `UNIPHYA/B` and `CHANNEL_XBAR` register matches. As predicted for a 241.5 MHz link (below the 340 MHz HDMI scrambling threshold), DVI and HDMI use the SAME PHY. ATOM transmitter `#76` was NEVER the answer — it stays gated for console safety, not because it is a lead. Do not re-open the transmitter/PHY bring-up as the audio fix.
- FALSIFIED — 'THE ANSWER IS A REGISTER VALUE agnos GETS WRONG.' The register-VALUE class is exhausted, PHY included. The comprehensive 40-register agnos-vs-amdgpu diff came back 35 exact matches and 5 differences that are all measurement-or-intent (`HDMI_ACR_STATUS_0` read-only measured CTS, `HDMI_ACR_48/44/32_0`, deliberate `AFMT_GENERIC_0` AVI). There is NO readable register whose value gates the audio.
- FALSIFIED — 'THE SILENCE IS A MISLABELED REGISTER OFFSET, like the P4 display bug' (where `0x607` was labeled SURFACE_PITCH but was really `EARLIEST_INUSE_C`, real pitch `0x603`). An INDEPENDENT audit of every audio-block offset in `gpu_regs.cyr` against `dcn_2_1_0_offset.h` + `dce_11_0_d.h` (Linux v6.6, curl'd raw) returned 105/105 correct, 0 mismatch, including every transmission-gating register the hypothesis named and the DCCG audio-DTO block. Corroborated by iron reads that only parse if the offset is right. The offset axis is RETIRED — no `gpu_regs.cyr` change is warranted.
- FALSIFIED — 'THE DCCG SYMCLKA RE-PRIME (abs `0x159 = 0x000d000d`, `0x15A`, `0x15B`, `0x15C`, `0x176 = 0x1111`) IS THE MISSING HDMI SYMBOL CLOCK.' Killed by an in-boot A/B on burn 11 (1.55.24, `audio_re_11.txt`), two clean passes: pre-write all zeros -> apply -> restore -> apply again. THE WRITES LAND AND THE SOUND DOES NOT CHANGE, same boot, one sink state. Corroborating kills: (a) abs `0x159` reads 0 on a LIT, WORKING display, so it cannot be DIG1's symbol-clock enable; (b) the symbol clock was ALREADY ON in every silent burn — agnos's own probe printed `gpu: audio probe dig1 fe=1000000 be=101` byte-identically at line 160 of all eight burn logs, where `fe` bit24 = `DIG_SYMCLK_FE_ON` and `be` bit8 = `DIG_SYMCLK_BE_ON` (`gpu_regs.cyr:186`, `:456`), read-only hardware acks. DELETE the block; it is a proven no-op.
- FALSIFIED — 'BURN 10 SHOWED THE SYMCLK WRITE ARMED THE SINK (shutdown pop + noise floor).' Retracted the same day it was written (2026-07-19) and then fully killed. Three falsifiers: (1) the symbol clock was already on, measured, in every silent burn; (2) the shutdown release pop was FIRST HEARD at `audio_re_5` / 1.55.20 — five burns and four cuts BEFORE the write — and its precondition (`AFMT_RAMP` = 0/0/0/0) was restored at 1.55.21 and held through burns 7-10, so burn 10's pop was the PREDICTED consequence of three-burn-old state; (3) ZERO instruments moved — burn 8 (silent) and burn 10 ('armed') are byte-identical on `DIG_FE_CNTL 1000100`, `DIG_BE_CNTL 10030200`, `AFMT_CNTL 101`, `AFMT_STATUS 40000010`, `HDMI_CONTROL 10019`, `HDMI_DB_CONTROL 1000`, `HDMI_ACR_STATUS_1 1800`, and the write is five BLIND absolute stores with no pre-read/readback/dump row (`gpu.cyr:2985-2989`). Operator testimony 2026-07-19 removed the last support: asked whether burns 7/8/9 had an audible noise floor he answered 'there were a few burns in cycle that exposed the noise floor; but can't recall at which ones' — the noise floor RECURS independent of the write. Do NOT cite burn 10 as a breakthrough anywhere.
- FALSIFIED — 'THE AUDIO READ CLOCK IS STALLED / THE CLOCK CLASS IS THE PROBLEM.' Killed by the N-doubling experiment on burn 11: `HDMI_ACR_N_48` 6144 -> 12288 moved measured CTS `241502 -> 483006 -> 241502`, exactly 2x, while tracking the pixel clock to 11 ppm. The CTS engine counts TMDS clocks GATED BY THE AUDIO CLOCK, so a stalled read clock cannot produce a stable, pixel-tracking, precisely-doubling value. THE WHOLE CLOCK HYPOTHESIS CLASS IS DEAD.
- FALSIFIED — 'THE PAYLOAD IS DIGITAL SILENCE / SOMETHING ZEROES THE SAMPLES BETWEEN FORMATTER AND WIRE.' Retracted 2026-07-19: the calibrated tap-identity experiment established that digital silence reads exactly `0x000000` at BOTH taps, and burn 11 reads `tap0=249f2f`, `tap1=b9b93c`. CONTENT SURVIVES TO THE LAST OBSERVABLE STAGE — do NOT go hunting a zeroing bug. Only the narrower amplitude question survives (taps are blind below ~-48 dB).
- FALSIFIED — 'THE ACR/CTS PATH IS INERT BECAUSE `ACR_SOURCE=0` MEANS THE PROGRAMMED CTS IS UNUSED.' Killed on iron at 1.55.26 (`audio_wk2_1`, `prior-art/hdmi-acr-cts-burn-tap1-zeroed-iron-0720.txt`): forcing `HDMI_ACR_CTS_48/44/32_0` to amdgpu's literal `0x3AF5C000` (241500) ZEROED tap1 — burn 11 had `tap0=249f2f tap1=b9b93c` (both content), this burn had `tap0=965cfd` and tap1 saw samples that are SILENCE — and `HDMI_ACR_STATUS_0` changed `3af5e000 -> 3af5c000`. The months-old inertness assumption is dead: the programmed CTS value reaches the formatter and changes its output. NOTE the direction — it made things WORSE, and the sink stayed silent, so this does not reopen the register-value class.
- FALSIFIED — 'COPY amdgpu's CTS LITERAL TO AVOID DIVERGENCE.' Backwards reasoning. amdgpu wrote 241500 because amdgpu's OWN clock was 241500 at capture time; agnos's real pixel clock is 241503 kHz (`display pixel clock 241503`). A CTS that does not match the actual TMDS clock breaks the sink's audio-clock regeneration / sample alignment -> null cells, which is exactly what zeroed tap1. Any future CTS test must use agnos's OWN measured clock, not amdgpu's number.
- FALSIFIED / CANCELLED — 'THE DCCG AUDIO-DTO READ-CLOCK DISCRIMINATOR (read DTO0 accumulator/PHASE twice, see if it advances).' Verified 2026-07-19 against `dcn_2_1_0_sh_mask.h` + `dce_audio.c`: THERE IS NO ACCUMULATOR. `DCCG_AUDIO_DTO0_PHASE` and `_MODULE` are each one 32-bit field bearing the register's own name (`..._PHASE__DCCG_AUDIO_DTO0_PHASE_MASK 0xFFFFFFFF`) — numerator/denominator of a fractional divider, pure config. `dce_aud_wall_dto_setup()` only ever `REG_UPDATE`s them; nothing in amdgpu reads them back. Iron agrees: `DTO0_PHASE` reads `0x3a980` in EVERY agnos burn AND on amdgpu's audibly-playing link. Zero information in either branch — a textbook echo register. DO NOT WRITE IT, DO NOT BURN IT. (Structural confirmation: where AMD does have a running DTO accumulator they expose it — `DCCG_GTC_CURRENT` 0x0063 BASE_IDX 1; the audio DTO has no analogue, the DCCG audio block is exactly five config registers.)
- FALSIFIED — 'SET `DTO2_USE_512FBR_DTO` (bit20).' In `dce_aud_wall_dto_setup()` the HDMI branch writes only `REG_UPDATE_2(DTO0_SOURCE_SEL, src_sel, DTO_SEL, 0)` then MODULE then PHASE and never touches 512FBR; only the DP/else branch sets it. Iron confirms: the audibly-playing HDMI link reads `DCCG_AUDIO_DTO_SOURCE = 0x00000000`, all three 512FBR bits clear. Source and silicon agree.
- FALSIFIED — 'AVMUTE IS HOLDING THE SINK MUTED.' Burn 11: `HDMI_GC = 4` vs amdgpu-playing `0x00000004`, and `HDMI_ACTIVE_AVMUTE = 0` on BOTH. Ruled out free.
- FALSIFIED — 'THE IEC-60958 NON-AUDIO BIT IS SET.' Channel status reads normal: L=1 / R=2. Ruled out.
- FALSIFIED — 'THE TRANSMITTER ENABLE IS A DMCUB COMMAND WE CAN SUBMIT (the T-series, Path A attach-the-live-ring / Path B PSP-load-the-firmware).' Killed by the T2 read-only burn (`audio_re_8`, 2026-07-18): `DMCUB_CNTL=0x20000` (SOFT_RESET set, ENABLE clear), `SCRATCH0=0`, `INBOX1 base/size/wptr/rptr all 0` — the GOP's DMCUB is HELD IN RESET with no ring and no firmware, yet the display is up. DMCUB is OS-driver-loaded and always dormant at boot; the boot-native transmitter path is HOST-ATOM. The entire T1-T4 ladder is retired — DO NOT IMPLEMENT IT.
- FALSIFIED — 'CAPTURE-AND-REPLAY THE PHY BRING-UP MMIO SEQUENCE (P5a/P5b/P5c/P5d).' P5b, source-verified against `dcn_2_1_0_offset.h`, showed the block P5a isolated as 'the RDPCS/DPCS PHY' (abs `0x3D3D-0x3DCE`) is actually `mmHUBPREQ3_*` page-flip + cursor programming — a desktop compositor idle-flipping the framebuffer. The 'cycling per-lane values' were front/back `DCSURF_PRIMARY_SURFACE_ADDRESS` alternation and the ~207x repeat was ~207 vblanks. The REAL RDPCS/DPCS PHY registers (abs ~0x5DE8-0x5EF0) got ZERO writes in the whole capture, because the genuine HDMI transmitter enable is `link_transmitter_control` -> an ATOM/DMCUB firmware call whose PLL-lock/calibration run inside firmware, invisible to any MMIO trace. THERE IS NO TRANSMITTER MMIO SEQUENCE TO CAPTURE OR REPLAY.
- FALSIFIED — 'THE SMU/PMFW GATES THE TRANSMITTER.' Airtight: the Renoir VBIOSSMC message enum contains no transmitter/DIG/PHY message, and agnos already sends the only relevant one (`UpdatePmeRestore`).
- FALSIFIED — 'ATOM transmitter `#76` with `phyid=0` (UNIPHYA) is the safe/correct phy.' The GOP-DVI dump shows `RDPCSTX1_RDPCSTX_CNTL = 0x00d1f000` (data-lane bits set) => the LIVE path is PHY B / phyid=1, so agnos's `#76 phyid=0` was wrong. And `RDPCSTX0` is ALSO fully powered (`PHY_CNTL0=0x30300204`, `PHY_CNTL6=0x001d3333`, `UNIPHYA_LINK_CNTL=0x01000100`), which is why phyid=0 blanked the console anyway — it power-cycled live silicon.
- FALSIFIED as a confound — 'BURN 10 WAS A TWO-VARIABLE EXPERIMENT (added SYMCLKA and dropped the live ATOM encoder path).' Burns `audio_re_3` through `_8` ran NEITHER ATOM nor SYMCLKA and were ALL silent, so removing ATOM merely returns the system to a documented-silent baseline; burn 10 is 'burn 8 + SYMCLKA', one variable. The live ATOM run left zero residue (burns 7/8/9/10 report identical DIG/AFMT/Azalia values including the one register ATOM transiently cleared). The real uncontrolled variable was the ORACLE (no ear record for burns 7/8/9, unrecorded monitor volume, and two uncaptured PHY-blanking burns in the 1.55.23 window that tore down and re-established the HDMI link between the silent baseline and the 'arming' burn — sink amp/mute/mode state is SINK-LATCHED and no agnos instrument can see it).

### From the HDMI-audio physical model work
- KILLED — 'MMIO is sufficient; no firmware seam needed, no DMUB, no ATOM' (this doc's headline conclusion, argued from pre-DMUB DCE prior art and from ATOM TransmitterControl being CPU-interpreted MMIO). Evidence: the DIG_ENABLE + AVMUTE edges it prescribed shipped and burned SILENT; the DCN audio register class was then exhausted byte-for-byte against amdgpu-playing and remained silent; agnos went on to build a sovereign ~600-700-line Cyrius ATOM BIOS interpreter (`kernel/core/atom.cyr`), A2/A3 bit-correct on iron at 1.55.23. ATOM was exactly the seam this doc said was unnecessary. ⚠ Second-order: the successor 'firmware transmitter/PHY bring-up is the A4 answer' thesis was ITSELF later falsified at 1.55.27 by the byte-identical GOP-DVI-vs-amdgpu-HDMI PHY comparison — so neither this doc's conclusion nor its replacement is live.
- KILLED — Stage 1: 'the cold output-enable + AVMUTE SET->CLEAR edge is the fix.' Implemented in the tail of `gpu_hdmi_audio_enable` (gpu.cyr) as: SET AVMUTE + close the tap -> drop DIG_ENABLE -> hold ~120 ms (burns 14/15 had held only microseconds) -> raise DIG_ENABLE + poll DIG_SYMCLK_BE_ON -> settle -> re-latch channel status + re-open SAMPLE_SEND -> hold ~50 ms -> CLEAR AVMUTE last. Burned. SILENT. Decisive corroborating observation: the SCREEN VISIBLY BLANKED during the DIG_ENABLE drop, so the sink DID receive a genuine link event and still played nothing. Do not re-propose an output-enable or AVMUTE edge as the audio unblock.
- KILLED — 'ROOT CAUSE FOUND: feed-before-drain FIFO phase' (declared iron-confirmed 2026-07-16). The drain-before-feed reorder shipped at 1.55.19 and BOTH predicted instruments flipped exactly as forecast — AFMT_AUDIO_CRC tap1 went non-zero for the first time, and AFMT_STATUS went 0x41000010 -> 0x40000010 (bit24 AUDIO_FIFO_OVERFLOW clear, byte-identical to amdgpu-playing) — AND IT WAS STILL SILENT. Necessary, not sufficient. The bug was real; the diagnosis of it as THE root cause was wrong.
- KILLED — 'the link is still pure DVI, no data islands are reaching the sink.' Refuted by the 2026-07-14 green-screen: a sink can only mis-decode agnos's RGB pixels as YCbCr if it is receiving, parsing, and honoring agnos's AVI InfoFrame. The DIP engine runs and the sink is in HDMI receive mode.
- KILLED — 'Stage 4, a minimal ATOM interpreter, is a durable fallback and likely unnecessary; Report-7 argues strongly it is not needed.' Exactly inverted: it became the actual path. A1 (Python `scripts/atom-interp.py`, 1461 lines, verbatim port of amdgpu atom.c), A2 (`gpu_vbios_acquire()` via the ACPI VFCT table), A3 (`kernel/core/atom.cyr`) all landed, iron-proven bit-correct at 1.55.23 with the DRY trace matching the Python oracle exactly (encoder #4 = 5 writes; transmitter #76 = 21 reads / 17 writes / 5 delays).
- KILLED — Stage 2's hypothesis that the HDMI-path PCM is wrong amplitude/sign/packing (a separate HDA controller/stream from the working analog jack). Exonerated three ways: (1) the analog jack plays the same tone perfectly; (2) a from-scratch Linux-userspace driver replaying agnos's exact hda.cyr feed on amdgpu's cold-modeset pipe PLAYED AUDIBLE SOUND; (3) the 1.55.21 magnitude burn drove -0.8 dBFS (amplitude 30000) and was silent. The feed, codec, DMA and whole audio program are exonerated.
- SUPERSEDED (not a hypothesis kill, but stop citing the number) — 'tap1 is frozen at 0xd97638 across every probe, so no real audio ever reaches the formatter.' True only pre-1.55.19. Burn 11 (1.55.24) read tap0=0x249f2f, tap1=0xb9b93c, and calibrated tap identity showed digital silence reads exactly 0x000000 at BOTH taps. Content survives to the last observable stage; there is no formatter->wire zeroing bug to hunt.

## ⚠ Standing iron hazards
Writes that blank, black or wedge the box; flags that must never be built; sequences with no recovery.

### From the compute (shipped 1.54.x) work
- **Stale-L2 trap.** Never CPU-pre-seed (UC) a buffer that the GPU is about to write and then use the read-back as an oracle — you get a ghost of the prior contents. Use a fresh, never-touched slot. This made the 1.54.18 burn unfalsifiable.
- **`ACQUIRE_MEM`/`GPU_CP_COHER_CNTL_TCWB = 0x00840000` INVALIDATES L2 (bit 23 `TC_ACTION_ENA`), it does not merely write back.** Any experiment that tries to *keep* L2 primed and then emits this packet destroys its own precondition. This exact mistake wasted the 1.56.4 burn (plan-S3 arm D).
- **WPTR submit order is load-bearing: LO then HI.** The CP latches on the HI write. Any other order = the CP never fetches, and the failure looks like a dead shader.
- **`mec_hdr != 0` is a broken ucode-presence guard** — the PSP path leaves the CP header-dump register at a `0xdefX_defX` sentinel (`0xdef0def0` observed), which defeats a `!= 0` test. Judge engine state on `CP_ME_CNTL`/`CP_MEC_CNTL` halt bits + `RLC_CNTL` + GRBM bit31 idle instead.
- **Engine-start ordering:** RLC first (`RLC_CNTL` bit0), then clear the CP-gfx and MEC halts (`me 0x15000000 -> 0`, `mec 0x50000000 -> 0`). Un-halting the CP over a dead RLC is not a tested path.
- **TMR placement:** the PSP TMR must be placed in the VRAM carveout via the GFXHUB FB-location registers. (Low kernel physical memory is PSP-DMA-reachable, which is what made `SETUP_TMR status=0x0` possible, but it is not where the TMR belongs.)
- **Byte-confirm every ISA/PM4 encoding before it rides a burn** — `llvm-mc -mcpu=gfx90c` as ground truth plus two adversarial re-derivations. The one time this doc asserted an encoding from memory (the `ACQUIRE_MEM` write-back claim) it burned a flash.
- **Diagnostic-ladder discipline: localize before fixing on iron.** No QEMU exists for AMD GFX/DCN, so every guess is a burn, and burns block the operator's only dev machine. Write each bite's driver code from the convergent prior art BEFORE the burn; the burn validates, it does not explore.
- **Build-size ceiling `scripts/check.sh:61` = `< 1,400,000` bytes**, with only 464 B of headroom as of 1.54.0. Compute register tables cross it. It is a discipline threshold, not a correctness bound — lift it rather than shrinking real code.

### From the display (shipped 1.55.x) work
- READ-HANG: reads of DCN registers behind a gated DISPCLK/DPPCLK domain, or in an unpowered pipe (OTG1-3 may be off), can HANG the box. Discipline that was proven safe: read `OTG_CONTROL` (0x1b41) FIRST and only touch HUBP registers for pipes that report enabled. Never blind-walk all HUBP instances on an un-probed part.
- BLACK-SCREEN PATHS on the `DIG_MODE` 2→3 (DVI→HDMI) commit — these three must be GATES INSIDE THE WRITER, never left to a separate burn to discover: (1) deep colour / scrambling enabled, (2) AVMUTE asserted, (3) any pixel format other than 24bpp RGB.
- SILENT-FAILURE ORDERING: writing `DTO0_MODULE` or `DTO_PHASE` before `DTO0_SOURCE_SEL`/`DTO_SEL` fails **silently** — hardware-enforced per AMD's `dce_audio.c` warning. No error, no log, just no audio. Always write source/select first.
- NEVER SNAP a measured display clock or refresh to a standard table or to the CVT 0.25 MHz grid. A snap can be **catastrophically wrong on an unlisted mode**, and on this exact link the grid fits a register-decode BUG 75x better than the truth. Refuse (cross-window spread > 200 ppm) rather than mis-snap — a silently-wrong DTO module is inaudible to test but wrong forever.
- NEVER derive raster timing, pixel clock or vblank period from `fb_width()`/`fb_height()` — those are the 800x600 SURFACE plane, not the 2560x1440 RASTER plane, and the DCN pipe scaler sits between them.
- QUIET-BOOT SCANOUT IS TILED/DCC: writing a LINEAR console into the GOP's surface with quiet-boot ON bands the glyphs (`fb_console.cyr:88-91`). Any new direct-FB writer must either honour the swizzle or run on agnos's own linear surface via the P1 re-point primitive.
- IRON-ONLY: QEMU emulates no AMD DCN. Every display bite must fall back cleanly to the direct-FB path off-iron; `gpu_display_probe` no-ops when the GPU/DCN is absent or the FB is not GOP-lit. Do not treat a green QEMU run as validation of any DCN write.

### From the shader (shipped 1.56.x) work
- MPCC_ALPHA_BLND_MODE mode 0 (PER_PIXEL) on the GOP's XRGB scanout surface, where the X byte is 0x00, makes the whole screen fully transparent = BLACK. Only mode 2 (GLOBAL_ALPHA), which ignores pixel alpha by definition, may ever be used against the GOP surface.
- `DOMAIN*_PG_CONFIG` sits at offset 0x0080 BASE_IDX 2 and NUMERICALLY COLLIDES with agnos's existing `GPU_R_OTG_PIXEL_RATE_CNTL = 0x80` at BASE_IDX 1. Writing with the wrong base POWER-GATES A LIVE PIPE. Name any new symbol distinctly (e.g. `GPU_R_DCPG_DOMAIN0_PG_CONFIG`) and assert the base in the comment.
- NEVER remove the pre-dispatch `ACQUIRE_MEM` invalidate (D-6). It is load-bearing twice: stale I$ on a rewritten shader VA, AND stale GL2 lines when a shader reads bytes CP-DMA just wrote MC-direct. S3 supplies the evidence (4096-of-4096 stale). The C2g-1 precedent for removing an 'obviously safe' cache op is EIGHT burns.
- There is NO GPU-side isolation: VM_CONTEXT0 paging is deliberately disabled, so a surplus lane's out-of-bounds store lands somewhere REAL in the carveout. Any deliberately-unguarded negative arm must be aimed at a sacrificial slot clear of the back buffers, the PSP TMR and the compute arena.
- DCN register writes must be INDEXED. A post-mortem defect in both D-lane tests: HUBPRET0 / MPCC0 / OPTC0 were written UNINDEXED while the surface-config register two lines away was correctly indexed.
- VOP3 and VOP3P take NO 32-bit literal on gfx9. Every selector/mask constant must be loaded with an `s_mov_b32` (SOP1) first. Named as the highest-probability first-burn bug in the packed path.
- There is NO scratch ring. `RSRC1`'s VGPR/SGPR fields must be recounted per kernel (`gpu_regs.cyr:1033-1035`); overflowing them is WRONG, not merely slow.
- `COMPUTE_PGM_LO = mc>>8` must stay exact with 256-byte-aligned resident slots (S12 multi-slot residency).
- Syscall #92 collides with Linux x86_64 `chown(path, uid, gid)`: arg1 is read as a user path pointer and arg2 (`len`) decodes as a uid, so a stray call on Linux is a METADATA WRITE if the path ever resolves. The file-level `#ifdef CYRIUS_TARGET_AGNOS` gate in `lib/syscalls.cyr` is load-bearing, and `scripts/agnos-crossbuild-gate.sh` coverage must include the row.
- Every new proof must use a FRESH, never-written arena slot pre-seeded with a sentinel distinct from both 0 and every expected value. Re-using a slot let a stale GL2 line FALSE-PASS a burn at C2g-1.
- A dispatch that 'completes' proves nothing, and neither does the done marker. Every oracle needs a lane/workgroup witness separating 'no store landed' from 'stored the wrong value' - the 1.54.17-19 lesson (the `nz` counter in `gpu_shader_dispatch2`).
- `s_mov_b64 exec,-1` must be issued FIRST on this raw non-HWS HQD path: the SPI hands the wave exec=0. `exec=-1` alone is correct ONLY for an exact 64-lane wave.
- A test whose oracle is a human looking at the screen must NEVER be a boot self-test - boot cannot ask a human anything. It belongs in a shell command / ring-3 binary. Five D-lane flashes would have been one.
- `gpu_blend_ok` gates NOTHING on the runtime path and MUST NOT - it is self-test-only (1.56.4 note). Do not wire it as a runtime gate by analogy with `gpu_matmul_ok`.
- A second full-screen 32bpp plane at 2560x1440@60 roughly DOUBLES scanout bandwidth against `DCHUBBUB_ARB_*` watermarks the GOP sized for ONE plane. Mitigation is a window-sized `RECOUT` (HUBP fetch scales with viewport - a 480x320 panel adds ~4% of a full plane).

### From the modeset / HDMI-audio investigation work
- DO NOT WRITE abs `0x52`. High-confidence analysis found bit8 is a pipe hold / soft-reset bracket around the WHOLE pipe (`0x110` open / `0x010` close in amdgpu's trace); latching it risks blanking the working scanout. It is NOT an audio lever, despite being one of the better-evidenced omissions in agnos's program.
- ATOM TRANSMITTER `#76` RUN LIVE BLANKS THE GOP PIPE NON-RECOVERABLY. It power-cycles the PHY (writes to `556F`, `5E03`, `5DF0`). Both PHYs are powered at boot, so there is NO 'safe' phyid — `#76` on EITHER phy is destructive. It stays behind `ATOM_RUN_TRANSMITTER` (default OFF) and is Linux-first only. Encoder-only `#4` is display-safe (and exonerated as silent).
- NO BLIND GPU REGISTER ACCESS — READS OR WRITES. Gated-clock domains hang on READ: a blind read-sweep of the OTG/DCCG blocks LOCKED archaemenid on 2026-07-17. Wrong PHY writes hang harder. Only header-verified offsets in known-ungated domains. NEVER a range sweep. (The one carved-out exception now proven safe is the `0x5Dxx`/`0x5Exx` PHY/RDPCS read domain.)
- The DMU/DMCUB region hangs on a blind read — use VERIFIED offsets only when touching `DMCUB_CNTL` / `DMCUB_SCRATCH*` / `DMCUB_INBOX1_*`. And a DMCUB soft-reset would tear down the GOP's DMCUB on this single-console box.
- LEAVE `HDMI_ACR_CTS_48/44/32_0` GATED OFF. Forcing amdgpu's literal `0x3AF5C000` (241500) on a 241503 kHz agnos clock ZEROED tap1 — it made the output strictly worse. Any future CTS write must use agnos's own measured clock (241503 -> ~`0x3af5cbc0`) or lock the ratio via `ACR_SOURCE`/N.
- DO NOT RE-OPEN THE AUDIO REGISTERS. Every one is byte-matched to amdgpu-playing and every offset is header-verified. Audio-register changes are not warranted; the fault is elsewhere.
- A NEW `#ifdef` MODE-FLAG NEEDS ITS `build.sh` DEFINE LINE, AND YOU VERIFY IT LANDED BY `cmp`-ING THE TWO BINARIES, NOT BY TRUSTING THE BURN TAG. `ATOM_DRY` was a silent no-op for TWO burns — both 'live' and 'dry' built byte-identical and drove the PHY, blacking the display twice. Build only via `BURN_*=1 sh scripts/burn/burn-prep.sh` and check the banner string.
- ATOM INTERPRETER LATENTS, cleared for #76/#4 but live for P6: (1) the recursion depth cap is 8 vs the amdgpu oracle's 32 — bump the cap AND the ws-pool level count TOGETHER if a P6 table nests deeper; (2) each ws slot is a fixed 512 B (128 dwords) but `ws_count` is a u8 (max 255), so a table with `ws_count > 128` overflows its slot into the next depth's — size slots to 1024 B (256 dwords) before running any large-workspace table.
- The operator's EAR is the only egress oracle (HDMI is transmit-only), and it is confounded by sink-latched state. Any audio A/B must be IN-BOOT (one flash, one sink state, one cable, one volume, one monitor power cycle) — cross-boot audio comparisons cannot control sink drift.
- Do not over-read a single amdgpu snapshot: `HDMI_CONTROL` bit3 differs between two audibly-playing captures (0716 vs 0720).

### From the HDMI-audio physical model work
- ATOM transmitter table #76 (`DIG1TransmitterControl(ENABLE)`) run LIVE blanks the GOP-lit pipe NON-RECOVERABLY — it power-cycles the PHY, writing 556F / 5E03 / 5DF0. Encoder-only table #4 (`DIGxEncoderControl` STREAM_SETUP) is display-safe (and exonerated as silent). #76 has already blanked this display twice.
- Stage 3's HPD bounce and UNIPHY/DCIO PHY transmitter power-cycle are CONSOLE-RISKY. Any attempt must stage a proven restore path first and be burn-isolated. Do not fold either into a general boot path.
- AFMT_AUDIO_CRC is an inadequate egress oracle: tap1 can read static IEC-60958 framing rather than audio (frozen 0xd97638 pre-1.55.19), and the CRC is AMPLITUDE-BLIND — the 1.55.17 -48 dB sign-folded packing bug lit it identically to a correct tone. Never declare a burn adjudicated on CRC movement alone.
- HDMI is transmit-only: there is no on-die wire observer. **The operator's ear is the ONLY true egress oracle — permanently, not provisionally.** ⛔ There is no "until an external capture instrument exists": such an instrument is prohibited (no box in the signal path, no hardware purchases) AND undrivable by agnos (zero isochronous USB support ⇒ no UVC/UAC device can be enumerated). See the tombstone under "From the HDMI-audio physical model work" below. The fix for un-adjudicable burns is **a negative control in the SAME boot**, not a better instrument — cross-boot comparisons cannot control for what changed between boots.
- Do not program HDMI_ACR_CTS_48/44/32_0 to amdgpu's literal 0x3AF5C000 (= 241500 << 12). Burned 1.55.26: still silent AND it made an instrument worse (tap1 went to zero). agnos's measured clock is 241503 kHz, not 241500. `HDMI_ACR_CTS` stays gated OFF.
- The DIG_MODE 2->3 flip and the whole HDMI_* register block are inert while DIG_MODE==2, so staging is free — but a LIVE poke is not a modeset. Every prior-art driver performs the audio bring-up inside a modeset, never as a live poke; treating a live flip as equivalent is what produced this doc's dead branch.
